# 摘要

| Ref | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [^1] | [Decoding the Past: An Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning Framework for Sex Attribution in Prehistoric Hand Stencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14539) | 本文提出了一种不确定性感知的深度学习框架，通过显式建模和聚合图像不确定性，显著提高了史前手印性别归属的准确性和跨种群泛化能力。 |
| [^2] | [Split the Labor: Separating Evidence Interpretation from Decision Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509) | 本文提出将证据解释与决策聚合分离，通过四字段证据元组设计接口，并揭示了计数尺度漂移这一失败模式。 |
| [^3] | [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505) | RecipeNet是一种层次化Transformer架构，通过编码步骤内字段交互和跨步骤顺序依赖，在配方数据表示学习上显著优于现有表格模型。 |
| [^4] | [Universal Thermodynamic Interatomic Potentials for Crystalline Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14502) | 本文提出了一种名为热力学原子间势（TIP）的新型模型，将原子间势扩展为热力学一致的吉布斯自由能模型，通过自动微分和通用势UMA实现，能高效计算晶体自由能并预测相变，从而加速材料发现。 |
| [^5] | [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498) | Rollplex通过将VLM后训练中的前缀计算与回放解码并行化，打破传统串行阶段限制，提升GPU利用率且不破坏同步同策略语义。 |
| [^6] | [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496) | 本文提出了一种两阶段跨表格数据生成框架，通过将异构表格标准化为统计表格，并利用扩散变换器模型生成合成数据，以解决多表异构数据生成的挑战。 |
| [^7] | [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492) | 本文提出sMuon方法，通过线性化和最小二乘法在低秩设置下近似Muon优化器，使LoRA等参数高效微调能与Muon结合，并仅用矩阵乘法实现高效计算，带来适度性能改进。 |
| [^8] | [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472) | 提出了一种基于线性规划的神经架构搜索框架，利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构建LP来计算架构更新方向，在保持参数最优性的同时提升泛化能力。 |
| [^9] | [Expected Free Energy-based Informative Path Planning for Robotic Mars Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14466) | 本文提出将期望自由能作为统一标准，用于预算受限的机器人信息路径规划，使自主机器人在探索未知环境时能同时构建准确信息图并高效定位高价值区域。 |
| [^10] | [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465) | 本文提出了一种在冻结语言模型的单次前向传播中同时实现回答和弃权的方法，通过训练小型网络重建引导前的残差来消除干扰，从而兼顾推理准确性和弃权能力。 |
| [^11] | [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443) | 本文提出了一种通用双层优化框架，通过连续松弛和三种可扩展方法（如神经元门控与混合激活）在多种架构中高效搜索紧凑且高性能的神经网络。 |
| [^12] | [Style or Signature? Artist-Disjoint Evaluation of Style Classification in Frozen Vision Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14435) | 本文发现，在艺术家分离的评估协议下，基于冻结视觉嵌入的艺术风格分类准确率显著下降，表明现有高准确率主要归因于对个体艺术家的识别而非真正的风格理解。 |
| [^13] | [Designing Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models: A Unified Path-Space View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14430) | 本文通过统一路径空间原则，揭示了扩散模型RL中反向轨迹与前向匹配方法的共同起源，并推导出方差缩减的等效形式，弥合了方法间的经验差距。 |
| [^14] | [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420) | 幂采样虽能提升轨迹质量，但因剂量和覆盖不匹配会导致下游推理性能下降，本文揭示了这一悖论并提出修复方法。 |
| [^15] | [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414) | CytoBERT是一个针对异质细胞计数数据的开源基座模型，通过自监督预训练解决了跨研究迁移难题。 |
| [^16] | [Online Inference in Distributional Temporal-Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408) | 本文为分布时序差分学习提供了在线推断的理论框架，证明了平均估计器和自助法的渐近正态性，并扩展到了非光滑泛函的局部推断。 |
| [^17] | [Offline Deep Q* Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401) | 本文提出一种新框架，利用扩散模型估计奖励和转移核以近似最优贝尔曼算子，并通过解耦算子估计与价值学习，在理论上证明了非渐近收敛速率。 |
| [^18] | [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385) | DeaMoE通过将专家分组并共享部门内参数，显著降低了小批量解码时的权重加载瓶颈，提升了MoE推理效率而不牺牲模型精度。 |
| [^19] | [Wrong but Useful: Trajectory Value Beyond Answer Correctness in Multi-Agent Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14375) | 本文提出了一种受控测量方法，证明在多智能体推理中，错误答案消息也可能包含对后续推理有用的轨迹价值，挑战了仅基于答案正确性过滤消息的传统假设。 |
| [^20] | [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373) | 本研究证明随机权重平均在增强数据上能提供超出其单独性能提升的等变性增强，且无需重复训练成本。 |
| [^21] | [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372) | 本文提出了一种名为“imposter”的自监督预文本任务，通过跨实体特征置换来捕捉物理一致性，并在ERA5-Land数据上验证了其在多项下游任务中的有效性，首次系统比较了地表建模中的SSL目标。 |
| [^22] | [Non-Shattering at and Above the Dynamical Temperature in the Spherical Pure p-Spin Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14369) | 本文证明了在球面纯p-自旋模型中，对于特定重叠参数范围，系统在动力学温度及以上不会发生破碎，部分解决了相关猜想。 |
| [^23] | [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367) | 本文通过分析14个事件日志，揭示业务流程中剩余时间呈强右偏分布，延迟检测的难度主要集中在长尾的大延迟案例上，而传统汇总指标掩盖了这一关键挑战。 |
| [^24] | [ATLAS: Discovering Agent Strategies through LLM-Guided Abstraction and Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352) | ATLAS通过结合轨迹抽象和自动机学习，从智能体行为轨迹中提取可解释的有限状态模型，以揭示其策略和模式。 |
| [^25] | [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349) | 本文提出一种零参数、无需训练的轨迹预测方法，通过状态条件转移采样和核检索，在数据稀缺时性能优于大型Transformer模型，并能用极少历史数据适应新区域。 |
| [^26] | [Clearing the Fog: Towards Installing and Refining Proactive Exploration Capabilities in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14339) | 本论文提出了一种名为\ours的方法，通过探索性数据构建和对比信号引导的强化学习，有效增强了LLM智能体的主动探索能力，克服了后见之明偏差并区分有效探索与冗余行为。 |
| [^27] | [CORAL: Curriculum-Optimized Reward Adaptation for LiDAR-Based Goal-Directed Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14332) | CORAL通过联合设计五阶段课程和阶段自适应奖励权重，使强化学习策略在长时程城市驾驶中按序学习从任务进展到安全合规的多种行为，且仅依赖紧凑的LiDAR直方图状态表示。 |
| [^28] | [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329) | 本文提出了首个涵盖准确性、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准性四轴的LLM法官基准，并引入可审计的校准评估器，以应对基于原则监管的复杂性。 |
| [^29] | [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319) | 本文首次证明了量子多臂老虎机和有限动作量子线性老虎机的极小极大遗憾下界，解决了是否存在与时间范围无关的遗憾问题，并提供了基于多项式方法和Remez型不等式的新证明技术。 |
| [^30] | [Intelligent Detection of Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) Metrics Based on 2D Floor Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14317) | 本文提出了一种基于Mask RCNN的神经网络模型，能从二维平面图中自动检测照明符号、识别灯具类型并提取相关文本，从而实现高效的楼层设计和电力需求估算。 |
| [^31] | [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303) | 提出了一种基于影响函数的威胁模型无关检测方法CodeSIFT，能有效识别代码生成提示批次中的污染，在中高注入率下AUROC高达0.98。 |
| [^32] | [Conditional Neural Optimal Transport for Predicting Cellular Phenotypes from Molecular Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14293) | 本文提出一种基于Monge-Gap正则化的分子条件神经最优传输模型，有效解决了从分子结构预测细胞表型的问题，克服了传统静态最优传输在大规模数据集上的局限性。 |
| [^33] | [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288) | 本文提出了多种新的凸损失函数用于SVM和神经网络，证明了它们是标准损失的推广，但实验结果显示这些新损失并未显著提升泛化性能。 |
| [^34] | [Pairton: Iterative Reconstruction of Short-Lived Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14278) | Pairton提出了一种基于图结构和迭代掩码预测的通用粒子重建框架，通过动态成对表示实现全局一致性，并在全强子t\bar{t}衰变中达到最先进性能。 |
| [^35] | [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264) | 本文提出MOBO-Merge，一种基于多目标贝叶斯优化的模型合并参数选择框架，能在有限评估预算下高效逼近帕累托前沿，并在多数基准测试中优于随机搜索。 |
| [^36] | [Meteorology-driven Causal Nowcasting of Fugitive Landfill Emissions Enables Proactive Public Health Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14254) | 该论文提出CAIRN框架，利用常规气象数据直接识别垃圾填埋场逸散排放的气象驱动因素，实现主动而非被动的公共卫生响应。 |
| [^37] | [Body size predicts how long ant workers live - but not how they age or how they die from heat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14245) | 本研究发现蚂蚁工蚁的寿命由体型大小预测，但衰老速度和热死亡脆弱性由昼夜节律生态位决定，而非体型。 |
| [^38] | [AutoSchema: Live Schema Grounding for Agentic Text-to-Sparql over Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14228) | AutoSchema提出了一种无需训练的实时模式锚定框架，使代理能直接从异构知识图谱端点获取模式证据，提升Text-to-SPARQL查询构建的准确性和适应性。 |
| [^39] | [Attributing Preprocessing Invariance in Spectral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14227) | 本文指出谱基础模型中的预处理不变性可能源于输入归一化本身，而非模型学习，并主张在评估时应将归一化单独作为基线。 |
| [^40] | [Connected Subspace Clustering: Hardness, a Scalable Heuristic, and an Application to Sea Level Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14215) | 本文提出并分析了连通子空间聚类问题，证明其NP难解性，并开发了可扩展启发式算法，应用于海平面大地测量中的连续区域识别。 |
| [^41] | [Training Fair Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14211) | 本文提出FairTFM，通过合成公平任务和梯度反转层架构，在表格基础模型训练中直接嵌入公平约束，实现单次前向传播中的公平预测，并在132个任务上验证了公平性与性能的平衡。 |
| [^42] | [Adaptive Protection for Evolutionary Feature Construction in Symbolic Regression with Application to Credit Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14209) | 本文提出了一种基于特征重要性度量的自适应保护机制，在符号回归进化过程中选择性保留重要构造特征，从而防止有效特征丢失并提升信用分类性能。 |
| [^43] | [FreeBalance: Pre-Routing Online Moe Load Balancing via Residual Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14205) | FreeBalance通过提前预测路由分布，将在线负载均衡与计算重叠，避免了迁移开销进入推理关键路径，从而显著提升MoE模型分布式推理效率。 |
| [^44] | [MINT: A Universal Zero-Shot Predictor for Transaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14198) | MINT通过将预训练的交易序列编码器与LLM连接，实现了对交易数据的通用零样本预测，克服了现有方法在信号利用和扩展性方面的限制。 |
| [^45] | [KV Cache Compression Through the Lens of Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14191) | 本文提出注意力感知变换编码（AATC），通过将KV缓存压缩问题建模为变换编码与反向注水，在考虑注意力误差传播的情况下优化比特分配，从而显著提升长上下文推理的内存效率。 |
| [^46] | [Revisiting Energy-based Tabular Anomaly Detection: Energy and Reconstruction are Complementary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14186) | 本文提出深度玻尔兹曼机的平均场能量与重构评分结合，在表格异常检测中优于同源基线，表现出能量与重构的互补性。 |
| [^47] | [Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14177) | 本文提出SGSAN模型，通过显式学习静态有向依赖图来增强交通流预测的可解释性，克服传统模型缺乏透明性的问题。 |
| [^48] | [Concept Guidance: Precise, Training-Free Latent Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14172) | 本文提出了一种无需训练的潜在控制方法CoG，通过量化层间概念互信息并增强相关层影响，实现精确的概念特定引导，解决扩散模型在连续控制和局部一致性上的缺陷。 |
| [^49] | [Classical Limits of Spectral Filtering in Quantum Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14169) | 本文证明了量子生成模型中谱过滤的经典极限，指出其效果在匹配采样成本下可由经典后处理等效实现，并给出了差距消失的充要条件。 |
| [^50] | [Removing Temporal Note Redundancy Improves Multimodal Reinforcement Learning for Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14157) | 本文提出了一种冗余感知的多模态强化学习框架，通过显式去除临床笔记中的时间性重复文本，提升了医学机械通气决策的状态表示质量和策略性能。 |
| [^51] | [Deep Reinforcement Learning solution for pickup and delivery routing problems with time window and capacity constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14156) | 首次将深度强化学习（改进JAMPR模型）成功应用于带容量和时间窗约束的取送货路径问题，实现了中小规模问题的快速最优解和大规模问题的快速次优解。 |
| [^52] | [Smart routes: a system for development and comparison of algorithms for solving vehicle routing problems with realistic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14140) | 该论文提出一个比较系统，证明在解决带时间窗的容量约束车辆路径问题时，深度学习与经典启发式算法在接近精确解的同时显著提升效率，尤其在大规模问题上优势明显。 |
| [^53] | [AgilePE: Autonomous UAV Pursuit-Evasion via Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14135) | 本文提出了AgilePE，一个通过自我对弈强化学习实现自主无人机追逃的完整系统，该框架将敏捷控制、竞争性策略优化和仿真到现实部署统一起来，实现端到端的敏捷机动。 |
| [^54] | [Overcoming Shortcut Learning in Graph Neural Networks through Active Explanation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14121) | XIGL是一种人机回环主动解释引导方法，通过检测并纠正图神经网络中的捷径学习，提升模型在分布外任务中的可靠性。 |
| [^55] | [From Fixed Grids to Moving Particles:A Transferable Latent Operator for Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14120) | 本文提出可迁移潜算子（TLO），通过解耦潜流动演化与坐标相关解码，使模型仅用欧拉数据训练后能零样本泛化到拉格朗日粒子预测，无需额外监督。 |
| [^56] | [Learning to Run Power Networks: Effective AlphaZero-inspired Topological Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14114) | 本文提出并优化了受AlphaZero启发的拓扑控制方法，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索实现电网主动管理，达到98.43%的峰值生存能力，显著优于传统方法。 |
| [^57] | [Fixed-Budget Gaussian Volume Encoding with Structure-Aware Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14112) | 本文提出一种固定预算下的结构感知高斯体编码方法，通过解析分配基元并直接细化，实现了高效的体数据压缩，显著降低编码时间。 |
| [^58] | [A Graph-Based Reinforcement Learning Framework for Structured Drift Diagnosis and Recovery in Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14109) | 本文提出一种基于图结构的强化学习框架，使用小型语言模型在外部恢复图中各节点进行专门化训练，实现自主LLM代理运行时行为漂移的步骤级检测、风险评估和恢复决策，而无需重新训练主模型。 |
| [^59] | [Forecast Collapse in Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14106) | 本文揭示了时间序列基础模型在预测股票收益时出现的“预测崩溃”现象，其根源在于低可预测性和逐序列目标设计，并指出了校准与排名之间的权衡关系。 |
| [^60] | [Sequence prediction under a lying oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14102) | 本文研究了在说谎预言机下通过对比查询进行序列预测的问题，针对随机和对抗环境分别提出算法，并实现了对数级别的遗憾上界。 |
| [^61] | [Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning for One-Warehouse Multi-Store Systems with Censored Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14096) | 本文提出了一种资源自适应原始-对偶学习框架，通过动态调整目标分配和对偶变量，在截断需求下实现了对数期望遗憾，显著优于现有平方根阶方法。 |
| [^62] | [Regime-Conditional Verification: Correctness Estimation for Adapting and Monitoring Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14089) | 本文提出了一种轻量级包装器RCV，通过估计分类器预测与部署者策略不一致的概率并选择性纠正，同时利用正确性估计检测分布漂移，实现了无需重训练即可适应和监控安全分类器，显著提升策略遵循度。 |
| [^63] | [When Denoising Hurts: Rethinking the Terminal Step of Diffusion Time Series Forecasters -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14067) | 本文发现扩散时间序列预测中过度去噪会引入统计漂移并损害性能，提出了一种无标签全局停止准则和伯努利时间步采样器，以在最佳点终止推理，加速推断并提升预测精度。 |
| [^64] | [Model-agnostic Retrieval-Augmented Extended Forecasting for time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14054) | 本文提出RAEF方法，通过输入空间直接检索和拼接式聚合，实现模型无关的低成本检索增强时间序列预测，兼顾效率与准确性。 |
| [^65] | [Adversarial Learning of Classifier-Free Guidance Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14038) | 本文提出通过对抗学习方式，动态预测扩散模型中的无分类器引导缩放，以改善文本到图像生成的保真度与对齐度。 |
| [^66] | [When Does More Correct Data Hurt? Insertion-Stability and the Limits of Dimension-Based Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14020) | 本文提出“插入稳定性”概念，揭示并非所有学习器都会因额外正确数据而受损，只有非插入稳定的学习器才会面临对数因子代价，从而填补了维度理论在最坏情况分析中的空白。 |
| [^67] | [Emergent Models: Intelligence from Tiny Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14019) | 本文提出涌现模型（EMs），一种基于简单动态基底（如元胞自动机）的机器学习范式，通过进化搜索训练，并证明其具有潜在通用性，能实现任何部分可计算函数，且偏向全局泛化。 |
| [^68] | [Buy the Rumor, Sell the News: When Is News Priced In?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14014) | 本文通过大规模金融新闻数据验证了“买传闻，卖新闻”的市场谚语，发现新闻相关的价格变动主要在发布前和发布时发生，而非之后，揭示了市场对公共信息的吸收速度。 |
| [^69] | [Identifiability and Order-Dimension Limits of In-Context Learning on Partial Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14004) | 本文提出了一种偏序集上上下文学习的理论，通过版本空间语义区分逻辑可辨识性、教学成本和结构复杂度，并证明了有限开放世界提示下查询完备性的三分法。 |
| [^70] | [Nanbeige4.2-3B on Apple Silicon: Fixing Deployment Bugs and Decreasing Looped Transformer Memory Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13987) | 本文修复了Nanbeige4.2-3B在苹果硅芯片上的五个部署错误，并提出分块预填充策略，将内存开销降低，使上下文宽度扩展2.7倍，从而使其可用于智能体任务。 |
| [^71] | [QUASAR: Lowering the Loss Floor of Quantization-Aware Training with Loss-Aware Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13966) | 本文提出QUASAR，一种在量化感知训练过程中持续进行轻量级损失感知重构的方法，以降低损失下限并提升低比特模型质量。 |
| [^72] | [Polar Code Based Federated Learning: Convergence Analysis and Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13961) | 本文提出了一种基于极化码的跨层联邦学习方案，利用不等错误保护特性优先保护重要量化比特，并给出了收敛性分析上界及资源分配优化。 |
| [^73] | [Deep Vision in Smart Manufacturing: MODERN Framework for Intelligent Quality Monitoring and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13937) | 本文提出了一种名为MODERN的深度学习框架，利用初始残差神经网络和迁移学习实现智能质量监控与故障隔离，并能在小样本条件下有效工作。 |
| [^74] | [Probabilistic indirect models for undrained shear strength: addressing significant data missing and variability with advanced imputation and machine learning techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13934) | 本研究提出了一种结合高级插补技术和机器学习（如PXGB和MHA）的概率间接模型，有效解决了不排水剪切强度预测中的数据缺失和变异性问题，提高了岩土设计准确性。 |
| [^75] | [Post-training Quantization for Hybrid Iterative Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13932) | HyGenQ是一种针对混合迭代生成模型的训练后量化框架，通过解决激活中的过度异常值和量化误差引发的放大异常问题，避免模型崩溃并保持生成质量。 |
| [^76] | [CForce: Boosting Parallel Decoding for dLLMs via Consistency Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13925) | 本文提出CForce，一种通过蒸馏强制早期预测与后期对齐的方法，显著提升扩散大语言模型在并行解码中的可靠性。 |
| [^77] | [High-dimensional nonparametric changepoint detection via low-rank degree-two density projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13922) | 本文提出了一种基于低秩二次密度投影的高维非参数变点检测方法，通过矩阵均值估计替代密度估计，并利用秩截断和CUSUM扫描实现高效检测，其复杂度随维度呈平方根增长。 |
| [^78] | [Hybrid Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Privacy-Aware Federated Biosignal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13914) | 本文提出并评估了一种混合量子启发柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（HQKAN），在联邦学习框架下用于隐私保护的ECG心律失常分类，相比传统MLP在通信效率和跨客户端分布鲁棒性上更具优势。 |
| [^79] | [MedMix: Specialization-Consistent Federated Sparse MoEs under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13911) | MedMix提出了一种语义对齐框架，解决了联邦多模态医学AI中因客户端和样本级模态异质性导致的稀疏专家混合模型路由分歧和专家专业化不兼容问题。 |
| [^80] | [Agentic Transaction: Towards ACID-Compliant Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13900) | 本文提出了一个ACID兼容的代理系统框架，通过重新定义原子性、一致性、隔离性和持久性为语义属性，以应对LLM代理在复杂任务中的可靠性和一致性挑战。 |
| [^81] | [Fashion Outfit Generation via Unified Sequential Composition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13888) | 本文提出了一种统一序列组合模型结合潜在扩展蒙特卡洛树搜索的方法，有效解决了时尚穿搭生成中的美学兼容性和组合搜索难题。 |
| [^82] | [Engineering Signals of Human-AI Collaboration in the Agentic Coding Era: A Longitudinal Analysis of 33,228 Pull Requests from vLLM and SGLang with Implications for Biomedical AI Agents and Bioinformatics Pipeline Developmen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13884) | 通过对vLLM和SGLang两个AI基础设施仓库的33,228个拉取请求进行纵向分析，本研究揭示了AI编码时代中团队开发速度和协作信号的显著变化，为生物医学AI智能体应用提供了实证参考。 |
| [^83] | [Variation Brownian Kernel Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13882) | 本文提出了变分布朗核阶梯（VBKL）框架，通过分离递归字典构建与线性变分叠加，证明了深度增长带来的表示复杂度提升，并给出了泛化理论保证。 |
| [^84] | [Geometric Filtering of LLM-Generated Samples for Few-Shot Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13866) | 提出一种基于嵌入空间几何距离的过滤框架，通过选择与真实样本几何一致的LLM生成样本来提升少样本文本分类性能，在多种设置下显著优于现有方法。 |
| [^85] | [Federated Prompt Learning: A Unified Framework, Empirical Analysis, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13844) | 本文系统综述了联邦提示学习（FPL）作为统一框架，整合联邦学习与大型语言模型，并深入分析了其动机、特性、权衡及未来研究方向。 |
| [^86] | [HI-MeshGraphNets: Efficient and Accurate Mesh-based Physics Learning with Hierarchical Multi-scale Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13827) | 本文提出HI-MGN，一种通过分层多尺度图粗化实现高效长程通信的网格物理学习新方法，显著提升了大规模网格模拟的效率和准确性。 |
| [^87] | [SPEAR: Structure Property Explainability with Attention Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13826) | 本文提出SPEAR框架，通过可学习温度和平滑性惩罚正则化注意力分布，显著提升光谱数据分析中模型预测的稳定性和物理可解释性。 |
| [^88] | [Trajectory Dynamics in Self-Supervised Learning Latent Space for Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13817) | 本文提出利用自监督学习潜在空间中的轨迹动力学来检测音频深度伪造，通过仅训练真实语音的LSTM预测器并结合监督阶段，在多个基准上取得优异性能。 |
| [^89] | [What preferences can - and cannot - predict in multi-agent online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13810) | 本文揭示了在一般博弈中，偏好图仅能预测子博弈的渐近稳定性，但在更复杂场景下，偏好稳定性与动态稳定性之间的等价关系失效。 |
| [^90] | [Stochastic Control Policies for Robust Molecular Transition Path Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13800) | 本文通过将基于展开的控制重新定义为路径空间提议分布学习，并引入两种随机策略（如FS-TPS），以提高分子过渡路径采样的优化鲁棒性和探索能力。 |
| [^91] | [Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing with Online Requests: Event-Driven Transformer--DRL and Rolling-Horizon Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13799) | 本文提出事件驱动Transformer-DRL框架解决动态多车场车辆路径问题，但实验表明简单最近可行启发式算法在路由质量和稳定性上优于学习策略，凸显了启发式方法的竞争力。 |
| [^92] | [Recent Advances in Deep Learning-Based Drug-Target Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13797) | 本文综述了基于深度学习的药物-靶点结合亲和力预测方法，比较了常见基准和指标，指出虽有性能优势但存在有效性和研究空白。 |
| [^93] | [On the Brittleness of Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Gaussian Process Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13793) | 本文揭示了最大似然估计在高斯过程超参数优化中的脆弱性，并提出基于理论指标的实用替代方案，以提高模型泛化能力。 |
| [^94] | [PPAPlace: Differentiable Cross-Stage Objectives for Chip Placement Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13790) | 该论文提出了一种可微分跨阶段目标方法，通过利用全局布线后阶段的高保真标签来优化芯片布局，解决了传统HPWL目标与最终布线后时序指标相关性低的问题。 |
| [^95] | [From Passive Delegates to Strategic Negotiators: Reinforcing Social Reasoning in Small Language Models with SocialRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13787) | 本文提出SocialRL训练方法，使小型语言模型（4B）在六个社会推理领域通过领域内训练达到或超越GPT-4水平，从而从被动代表转变为战略谈判者。 |
| [^96] | [CutClean: Neural Network Pruning for Privacy-Preserving Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13773) | CutClean通过在每个网络块添加辅助隐私头量化信息泄露，并应用递增稀疏性剪枝，在保持高稀疏性的同时显著减少隐私属性泄露。 |
| [^97] | [Amplified Does Not Mean Predictive: Reasoning Behaviors in Thinking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13760) | 本文发现思考模型在推理训练中放大的行为（如自我纠正）并非预测正确性的关键，而置信度校准等被忽略的行为才是提升准确性的核心，揭示了“放大-提升差距”。 |
| [^98] | [The Integer Alibi: Localizing Cross-Kernel Divergence in INT8-Quantized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13756) | 本文通过实验证明，在固定所有其他条件下，仅替换INT8线性内核（CUTLASS与Triton）会导致大语言模型推理输出完全不一致，并利用整数运算的精确性定位到差异源自内核实现而非累加器。 |
| [^99] | [Data-driven techniques for translational neuroscience and personalized neuro-health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13749) | 本文综述了数据驱动技术如何通过四个方法论支柱，推动个性化、机制驱动且临床可操作的脑健康模型发展，以应对神经退行性疾病的早期检测挑战。 |
| [^100] | [Does ISO-Grounded NFR Specification Improve LLM Code Generation? A Comparison of Rich and Structured Interventions against a Natural-Language Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13742) | 基于ISO的NFR规范丰富化能提升代码静态质量并降低对提示措辞的敏感性，但无法可靠改善功能正确性，甚至可能因防御性编码模式导致错误处理场景的测试通过率下降。 |
| [^101] | [GALA: Generation-Aware Cross-Modal Alignment for Text-to-Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13741) | GALA通过两阶段方法，将文本编码器与时间序列模型对齐到共享嵌入空间并优化生成损失，显著提升了文本到时间序列合成的性能。 |
| [^102] | [Building AI-Intensive Software with AI: Early Results and a Cautionary Tale on Measuring Development Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13730) | 该论文报告了一个AI辅助开发项目的成本测量案例，发现初始19.4倍成本比率因两个测量错误被高估约2倍，修正后为9.9倍，并强调成本测量中的潜在陷阱。 |
| [^103] | [Architecture and Affordances of PLAUD: Performative Latents and Unsupervised DDSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13724) | 本文提出了PLAUD系统，其核心创新在于通过架构决策（如变分DDSP、潜变量平滑和弯曲操作）而非事后设计，实现了现场电子音乐的表演性可供性。 |
| [^104] | [Capacity-Dependent Effects of Data Selection for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13721) | 高似然数据选择在推理微调中的效果随模型容量和训练时长变化，小模型受益于早期快速提升，但大模型可能更偏好低似然数据以获取长期增益。 |
| [^105] | [TRUE-Colon: Exposing a Consistent Transfer Asymmetry in Real-Time Polyp Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13711) | 本文提出TRUE-Colon标准化基准协议，揭示基于精选数据集训练的实时息肉检测模型在真实临床全流程中表现出一致的迁移不对称性，即性能显著下降，暴露了现有评估方法的局限性。 |
| [^106] | [SAGE: Surrogate-gradient Adaptation via Attention-Guided Entropy for Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13702) | SAGE通过基于自注意力熵的不确定性调制替代梯度斜率，在不改变推理架构的情况下提升了脉冲Transformer的训练效果和准确率。 |
| [^107] | [GRPO Beyond English: A Large-Scale Study of GRPO in Non-English and Multilingual Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13698) | 该研究通过大规模实验证明，非英语和多语言环境下的GRPO训练不仅与英语训练性能差距小，还能实现强跨语言迁移，但效果因模型和语言而异，并可能带来某些语言的性能退化。 |
| [^108] | [Language-Specific Gaps in AI Safety Training Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13695) | 该论文通过审计21个资源并比较不同语言切片，揭示了AI安全训练数据集中存在语言特定的质量缺口，尤其低资源语言（如豪萨语）的翻译质量显著低于标准。 |
| [^109] | [hint$^2$: Hierarchical World Models for Inference-Time Temporal Logic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13678) | 本文提出hint$^2$方法，通过层次化世界模型在推理时为短视界策略提供双重引导目标，以有效满足复杂线性时间逻辑规范。 |
| [^110] | [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676) | EEG-PRISM提出了一种通用方法，通过线性变换和反向传播规则，将EEG基础模型的归因分数映射到频域和源域等生理相关领域，无需修改模型即可增强临床可解释性。 |
| [^111] | [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675) | 本文回顾了2018至2026年间语言模型的飞速发展，指出能力提升与成本骤降并行，并揭示了顶级性能向任务定制化专业模型分化的趋势，同时展示了高级采样和置信度排序工具在实际任务中的显著效能。 |
| [^112] | [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668) | 本论文提出了一种名为QED的新方法，通过添加与键正交的查询派生擦除方向，显著提高了线性注意力模型在长上下文中的检索能力，并将可用上下文长度翻倍。 |
| [^113] | [What to Preserve, Where to Adapt: A Depth-Wise Analysis of Forgetting in Continual Gynecological Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13660) | 本文通过深度分析揭示，在持续妇科图像分割中，早期编码器和晚期解码器区域对遗忘影响最大，为设计更有效的保留策略提供了针对性指导。 |
| [^114] | [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652) | ORCA通过两阶段对比学习框架，在提高异常检测敏感度的同时，利用嵌入空间的可解释性解决了异常分数难以解释和与能量规模强相关的问题。 |
| [^115] | [Consistent Model Chasing Is Minimax Optimal: The Exact Value of Scalar Adversarial Adaptive Control under Large Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13651) | 本文精确求解了标量对抗自适应控制问题的极小极大值，证明其最优代价为 $1+\Delta$，其中 $1$ 为扰动固有代价，$\Delta$ 为参数不确定性导致的辨识尖峰代价，并给出了最优策略。 |
| [^116] | [Unknown Unknowns: Model Misspecification in Machine Learning for Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13633) | 本文探讨了物理机器学习中模型错误设定（即“未知的未知”）的挑战，提出稳健分析应吸收不相关错误设定，同时保持对潜在新发现的敏感性，并讨论了检测和缓解策略。 |
| [^117] | [Learning Unsteady Aneurysm Hemodynamics with Physics-Informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13629) | 提出一种M3PI-DeepONet架构，通过聚合注入策略融合多输入分支，首次结合逐层门控，用于高效预测腹主动脉瘤中的非定常血流动力学，如壁面剪切应力和压力分布。 |
| [^118] | [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628) | 提出了一种基于核风险敏感均值p次幂准则的鲁棒RVFL模型，并引入协同学习机制，以有效应对噪声标签和异常值，提升模型的稳定性与泛化能力。 |
| [^119] | [A Calibrated Test of Internal Action Maps: State Signals Without Global Affine Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13626) | 本文通过系统化的证据格测试表明，内部动作映射在无全局仿射闭合条件下仅部分可校准，且不同层级的映射能力存在显著差异，挑战了通用动作映射假设。 |
| [^120] | [Reward Machines for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13625) | 本文提出了一种基于自动机的新方法，为信号时序逻辑规范在强化学习中提供高效的内存机制，解决了鲁棒性奖励依赖历史导致状态空间爆炸的问题。 |
| [^121] | [Adjacency-Based Spectral Proxy Control of Mobile Communication Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13616) | 本文提出了一种基于邻接的谱代理控制方法，通过将Fiedler梯度控制器分解为局部交互和图嵌入，采用更易分布式估计的替代嵌入，从而在异构移动代理网络中实现高效的在线通信代理重定位。 |
| [^122] | [VoiceDesigner: Text-to-Voice Generation and Editing via Unified Diffusion Modeling and Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13613) | 本文提出VoiceDesigner，一个统一框架，通过混合数据管道和扩散建模，解决了文本到语音生成中多样性和编辑灵活性的关键挑战。 |
| [^123] | [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608) | 提出了一种基于缩放假设的无标签评估框架，利用更强教师模型的稀疏纠正来衡量持续学习驾驭系统的端到端改进效果，解决了网络安全中基准标签稀缺导致评估困难的问题。 |
| [^124] | [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607) | 本研究发现，在大语言模型版本更新下，没有任何单一信号能普遍预测样本级退化，信号有效性高度依赖具体任务类型。 |
| [^125] | [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606) | MobileMem是一个基于一年移动体验数据构建的基准框架，通过知识引导合成流水线生成时间一致的长期轨迹，支持多跳推理、知识更新和偏好推断，用于研究设备端长期记忆能力。 |
| [^126] | [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601) | 本研究通过对照实验发现，不确定性采样在标签噪声下的失败主要源于困难区域中错误位置的集中影响，而非单纯错误暴露数量的增加。 |
| [^127] | [Continual Evolution Strategies in Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13600) | 本文发现进化策略在持续控制任务中易受灾难性遗忘，而重放机制能有效缓解遗忘并促进正向迁移，但需权衡可塑性。 |
| [^128] | [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596) | 本文提出激活引导的剪枝合并框架（APM），无需训练即可通过任务条件激活图选择供体模型的关键部分并注入受体，实现跨模型规模的高效知识迁移。 |
| [^129] | [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590) | 本文提出MM-XGBoost方法，通过结合稳健回归的M估计和S估计损失函数，有效提升了XGBoost在存在异常值和杠杆点时的稳健性，同时保持了较高的预测精度。 |
| [^130] | [From Prediction to Intervention: Personalized Meal-Level Glucose Regulation via an LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13581) | 本文提出了一种结合生理感知血糖预测器和LLM驱动的两阶段餐食优化智能体的统一系统，实现了从个性化血糖预测到动态饮食干预的闭环调控。 |
| [^131] | [BCMT: Blockwise Causal Memory Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13578) | BCMT通过块状局部自注意力与可并行化的指数因果记忆机制，在无需全局注意力或学习记忆状态的情况下实现高效的长上下文建模，突破了二次复杂度瓶颈。 |
| [^132] | [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577) | 本文主张AI评估应从追求取代人类的自主性能，转向评估人机协作效能，以促进AI成为人类能力的补充，实现更优社会结果。 |
| [^133] | [BCIJelly: An integrated ecosystem for brain-computer interface research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13576) | BCIJelly是一个集成18个数据集、15个解码器和80个模块的Python生态系统，通过自动化架构搜索和LLM引导的闭环模式，以及toChip硬件部署流程，解决了BCI研究中数据、算法和部署碎片化的问题。 |
| [^134] | [Interactive Analysis of Global Explanations using Aggregated Class Activation Maps for Network Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13575) | 本文提出一个可视化交互系统，通过聚合类激活图生成全局解释，帮助分析网络流量分类中的分歧模式，并提取描述性规则以增强模型预测的可理解性。 |
| [^135] | [Think in Latent, Explain in Language: Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13570) | 本文提出了一种自解释潜在推理框架（SELR），通过单一模型同时实现高效潜在推理和可解释的语言解释，解决了现有方法在可解释性与效率之间的权衡问题。 |
| [^136] | [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567) | 该论文发现大型语言模型通过电路分析，在语言、形式推理、社会推理和物理推理等任务中自发形成与人脑相似的模块化认知架构，表明模块化可能是智能系统的普遍原则。 |
| [^137] | [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566) | 该论文指出，基于少量编码基准（如SWE-bench）的优化并不能提升通用编码能力，因为基准排名缺乏跨任务泛化性，需要多样化评估来验证真实能力。 |
| [^138] | [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562) | L-FNO通过结合洛伦兹谱核和似然训练目标，显著提升了随机事件动力学中罕见事件的预测准确性和校准性能。 |
| [^139] | [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341) | UltraIR是一个基于大规模模拟光谱预训练的红外光谱基础模型，通过仿真到现实的迁移学习，实现了从分子到复杂样品的高精度化学传感与分析。 |
| [^140] | [Learning Discrete Decisions for MIPs with Constraint-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13079) | 本文提出一种约束感知的图扩散模型（CGD），通过将可行性投影集成到扩散生成过程中，高效学习混合整数优化问题的离散决策，从而简化求解。 |
| [^141] | [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973) | 本文提出了一种基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，通过泛函中心极限定理支持，在不存储完整轨迹的情况下对分布强化学习中的QTD进行统计推断，显著减少了内存需求。 |
| [^142] | [Fast A/B/n Testing: Exact Multi-Policy Comparison via Tree-Coupled Feedback Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12831) | 本文提出了一种名为树耦合A/B测试（\TCAB）的新方法，通过树状结构共享反馈，在保持每个策略独立轨迹规律的同时，显著减少奖励查询次数，实现高效且精确的多策略比较。 |
| [^143] | [AI-Driven Multiscenario Interest Rate Forecasting: A Proof of Concept for Banking Asset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12424) | 本文提出了一种结合贝叶斯向量自回归与人工智能技术的多视角利率预测原型系统，在银行实际测试中实现了更精准的预测，并创新性地整合了多种预测方法以支持资产-负债管理决策。 |
| [^144] | [From Recoverability to Functional Use: Certifying Temporal Reports in Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10433) | 该论文提出了时间序列预测中时间报告认证的三阶段框架，并揭示了可恢复性与预测性能之间的尺度差异，从而指导系统评估。 |
| [^145] | [Test-Time Scaling for CAD Generation via Verifier-Free Consensus Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09706) | 我们提出了一种无验证器的共识选择方法，通过采样多个CAD程序并选择与其余候选最一致的编译3D模型，无需额外验证器即可提升文本到CAD生成的测试时性能。 |
| [^146] | [From Recovery to Drop-off: How Action Post-training Reduces a VLM's Late-Layer Depth Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08904) | 本文发现动作后训练导致VLM深层深度解码能力显著崩溃（“悬崖”），并因果证明这源于后期层MLP干扰，而非注意力机制。 |
| [^147] | [Distribution-Free Conformal Prediction for Steel Fatigue Strength: Marginal Validity Is Not Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07589) | 本文首次将保形预测应用于钢疲劳强度预测，揭示了边际覆盖率不足以确保所有预测区域的可靠性，并指出在特定子区域内覆盖率显著下降。 |
| [^148] | [From Non-Convex Self-Concordant Regularization to Scalable Quasi-Newton Training of PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04206) | 本文提出SCORE方法，通过自和谐启发的准牛顿减量联合控制步长选择和割线几何移位，从而提升PINN训练的可扩展性和稳定性。 |
| [^149] | [Latent Reward Registers for Diffusion Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03929) | 本文提出潜在奖励寄存器机制，通过向冻结的扩散变换器附加可学习寄存器标记，从中间噪声潜在变量直接估计终端偏好，生成密集可微分的奖励场，并利用在线策略蒸馏实现高效的扩散模型偏好对齐。 |
| [^150] | [A Direct Route to Markov Chain Convergence via Asymptotic Equivalence with the Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03353) | 本文提出并证明了一种基于与目标测度渐近等价的充分必要条件，用于判断马尔可夫链的收敛性，并在多种典型场景下验证了该准则的有效性。 |
| [^151] | [Subtype Robustness Is Not Just Accuracy: Calibration Under Unseen Subtype Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00928) | 本研究首次系统揭示了在未见子类型转移下，模型校准性会崩溃，导致过度自信，且该现象独立于一般准确性损失，无法通过基于已见子类型的重校准完全修复。 |
| [^152] | [A Negative-Control Protocol for Clinical EEG Foundation-Model Benchmarks: Dataset Identity and External-Cohort Stress Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24519) | 该论文提出了一种阴性对照协议，证明脑电图基础模型的性能提升可能源于数据集身份识别而非实际泛化能力，并通过外部队列压力测试验证了这一现象。 |
| [^153] | [Debiasing Text-to-Image Evaluation via Implicit Cultural Alignment Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15740) | 本文提出了一种基于轻量级多模态大语言模型的隐式文化对齐奖励模型，通过跳跃连接交叉注意力机制，解决了文生图评估中文化偏差和实时扩展性问题。 |
| [^154] | [ShortOPD: Recovering Pruned LLMs with Short-to-Long On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13124) | 本文提出ShortOPD，通过短到长的在线策略蒸馏，有效恢复结构化剪枝后的大语言模型在自由生成任务上的性能，避免了长滚动中重复后缀导致的训练浪费。 |
| [^155] | [RUBRIC: Realism--Utility Balanced Ranking for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09816) | RUBRIC是一个与生成器无关的过滤框架，通过现实性与效用的平衡排序，将合成样本选择优化为质量优先问题，从而减少低质量候选样本对决策边界的干扰，提升不平衡分类的泛化性能。 |
| [^156] | [Neural Operator-enabled Topology-informed Evolutionary Strategy for PDE-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07682) | 本文提出了一种将神经算子与进化策略相结合的拓扑信息进化方法，在紧凑潜在空间中进行全局优化，实现了高效且可迁移的偏微分方程约束逆向设计。 |
| [^157] | [Dissociating the Internal Representations of Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07003) | 本文通过区分事实型和观点型谄媚子类型，揭示了不同大语言模型在内部表征上对谄媚行为的异同，并利用这一发现优化了表征干预策略。 |
| [^158] | [$\mu$Flow: Leveraging Average Images for Improving Generalisation of Deepfake Faces Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30528) | 本文提出μFlow，一种仅用真实图像训练的单类深度伪造检测器，通过平均图像放大生成痕迹并利用归一化流对齐特征，显著提升跨生成器类别的泛化能力。 |
| [^159] | [When Prices Double in a Week: Forecasting of Agricultural Volatility in Import-Isolated Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29248) | 本研究提出一种结合供应链特征和种植季节建模的机器学习框架，用于预测进口隔离市场中蔬菜价格的剧烈波动，其中季节特定模型显著提升了预测精度。 |
| [^160] | [Event-Conditioned Diagnostics of Kinematic, Contact, and Object-Permanence Fields in Passive Object-State World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28455) | 本文提出了一种受控诊断协议，用于揭示被动对象状态世界模型中事件条件如何影响潜在物理结构的编码与预测功能。 |
| [^161] | [It's Much Easier for Neural Networks to learn Game of Life Dynamics with the Right Activation Function: Polynomial Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23587) | 本文表明，使用二阶多项式激活函数（如多项式Kolmogorov-Arnold网络）能显著提高最小神经网络学习康威生命游戏动态的能力，优于默认的ReLU激活函数。 |
| [^162] | [Breaking Chains with Trees: Model-Parallel Deep Learning with $\mathcal{O}(\log N)$ Time Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21497) | TreeProp通过将网络层组织成树状结构，实现了深度神经网络训练的模型并行，将计算复杂度从顺序依赖降至\(\mathcal{O}(\log N)\)，这是首个突破反向传播顺序限制的学习算法。 |
| [^163] | [Cross-Calibrated Confidence Fields for Local Risk Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19147) | 本文提出了一种交叉校准置信场方法，能够同时覆盖局部更新空间中所有更新的总体风险变化，从而支持更新比较与选择，并在有限样本下保证联合覆盖性。 |
| [^164] | [Point-Cloud-Assistant Localized Statistical Channel Prediction by Tangent Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18734) | 本文提出点云辅助的切向高斯溅射（PC-TGS）框架，首次利用LiDAR几何信息与稀疏无线电测量结合，实现未测量户外位置的角功率谱预测，突破了传统局部统计信道建模的局限性。 |
| [^165] | [Your Privacy My Cloak: Backdoor Attacks on Differentially Private Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17035) | 本文提出RING攻击，利用差分隐私的掩盖效应隐藏恶意更新，使DP-FL中的现有防御失效，并实现强后门注入。 |
| [^166] | [RL-Index: Reinforcement Learning for Retrieval Index Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16316) | 本文提出 RL-Index 框架，通过强化学习在索引阶段生成推理依据，将检索推理从查询端转移到索引端，以降低延迟并提升检索效果。 |
| [^167] | [OCOO-T : A Simple and Scalable Virtual Cell Model for Transcriptional Perturbation Response Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12838) | OCOO-T提出了一种极简的基于流匹配的虚拟细胞模型，仅用普通Transformer处理连续基因表达，简化架构并提升可扩展性，用于高效预测转录扰动响应。 |
| [^168] | [Revisiting the shutdown problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08296) | 本文重新审视了AI关闭问题，指出现有论证缺乏充分假设和证据，且相关技术方案以高性能代价为安全税，建议研究应聚焦于这些关键假设。 |
| [^169] | [Learning Transfers: Kan Extensions for Neural Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07627) | 本文通过范畴论中的Kan扩展形式化定义了表示迁移所需保持的结构，并提出了一种新的迁移差异度量，以任务改变所强制的不变量为目标进行评分。 |
| [^170] | [Multimarginal flow matching with optimal transport potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05327) | 本文提出了一种结合动态最优传输势的多边缘流匹配方法，通过软约束中间边缘分布，实现高效且无模拟的时空动态学习。 |
| [^171] | [TIDE: Proactive Multi-Problem Discovery via Template-Guided Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04743) | TIDE提出了一种模板引导的迭代框架，通过分轮发现隐藏问题并基于已有发现扩展覆盖，从而主动揭示上下文中多个共存且数量未知的问题。 |
| [^172] | [Supervised Training Rapidly Degrades Early Visual Cortex Alignment Across Biologically Plausible Learning Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30556) | 该论文发现，监督训练在所有测试的生物合理学习规则下都会降低早期视觉皮层（V1）的表征对齐度，且随机未训练网络反而匹配或超越训练网络，挑战了学习改善大脑对齐的传统假设。 |
| [^173] | [PhoneWorld: Scaling Phone-Use Agent Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29486) | PhoneWorld通过真实GUI轨迹自动构建可扩展的手机使用环境，解决了移动代理训练与评估中环境构建的瓶颈。 |
| [^174] | [Localization then Neutralization: Gradient-guided Token Suppression against Visual Prompt Injection Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25194) | 本文提出梯度令牌掩蔽（GTM）方法，通过梯度分析定位并掩蔽对抗图像中的关键令牌，从而有效防御视觉提示注入攻击，并提供了理论保证。 |
| [^175] | [Coupling-Robust Accuracy in Multiphysics Physics Informed Neural Networks via Kronecker-Preconditioned Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23391) | 本研究通过Kronecker预条件优化器（SOAP）实现块对角高斯-牛顿预条件，从理论上证明并解决了PINNs在多物理场耦合中精度随耦合强度退化的问题，提供了无需逐例调参的通用方案。 |
| [^176] | [Cross-Species RSA Reveals Conserved Early Visual Alignment but Divergent Higher-Area Rankings Across Human fMRI and Macaque Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22401) | 该研究通过跨物种表征相似性分析，发现人类fMRI与猕猴电生理在早期视觉处理上存在保守对齐，但高级脑区的排名在不同物种间存在显著差异。 |
| [^177] | [OlmoEarth v1.2: A more efficient family of OlmoEarth models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20804) | 本文通过对OlmoEarth模型家族进行改进，在保持性能的同时，显著降低了训练和推理的计算成本。 |
| [^178] | [Inpainting physics: self-supervised learning for context-driven fluid simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08832) | 本文提出将稳态CFD推理重新定义为修复问题，通过自监督学习速度场先验并在推理时施加边界约束，结合局部邻域分词器实现大规模三维网格的高效流体模拟。 |

[^1]: 解码过去：一种用于史前手印模板性别归属的不确定性感知深度学习框架

    Decoding the Past: An Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning Framework for Sex Attribution in Prehistoric Hand Stencils

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14539](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14539)

    本文提出了一种不确定性感知的深度学习框架，通过显式建模和聚合图像不确定性，显著提高了史前手印性别归属的准确性和跨种群泛化能力。

    

    arXiv:2608.14539v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：确定创造旧石器时代晚期手印模板的个体的生物学性别仍然是一个具有挑战性的问题，原因是缺乏真实标签、当代与史前群体之间的种群差异，以及图像退化引入的不确定性。传统的形态计量方法在性别间存在高结构重叠、跨种群泛化能力差以及主观特征工程等问题。本研究提出了一种用于史前手印模板性别归属的不确定性感知深度学习框架，该框架在整个分析流程中显式建模、传播和聚合不确定性。该方法结合了双图像处理、双轮廓提取、结构化轮廓增强、模型架构多样性和基于集成的决策聚合。该流程为每个模板生成十二种可能的轮廓实现，以捕捉边界不确定性，这...

    arXiv:2608.14539v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Determining the biological sex of the individuals who created Upper Paleolithic hand stencils remains a challenging problem due to the absence of ground truth, population differences between contemporary and prehistoric groups, and the uncertainty introduced by image degradation. Traditional morphometric methods suffer from high structural overlap across sexes, poor cross-population generalizability, and subjective feature engineering. This study presents an uncertainty-aware deep learning framework for sex attribution in prehistoric hand stencils that explicitly models, propagates, and aggregates uncertainty throughout the analytical pipeline. The methodology combines dual image processing, dual contour extraction, structured silhouette augmentation, model architectural diversity, and ensemble-based decision aggregation. The pipeline generates twelve plausible silhouette realizations per stencil to capture boundary uncertainties, whic
    
[^2]: 分工协作：将证据解释与决策聚合分离

    Split the Labor: Separating Evidence Interpretation from Decision Aggregation

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509)

    本文提出将证据解释与决策聚合分离，通过四字段证据元组设计接口，并揭示了计数尺度漂移这一失败模式。

    

    arXiv:2608.14509v1 公告类型：新 摘要：要求语言模型从多个来源得出结论的系统通常会将它们拼接成一个提示。这混淆了两个需求不同的操作。解释来源需要能力和上下文。整合解释则需要固定的算术、跨实例的可比性，以及返回空值的选项。一旦分离，设计问题就变成了它们之间的接口。我们提出了一个四字段证据元组（假设、可靠性分桶、理由、出处），并表明固定它可以确定这两个部分。这种分离还揭示了此类系统在组合时的一种失败模式，我们称之为计数尺度漂移。对未归一化权重的总和进行阈值化恰好是后验阈值化，但操作点会随着所咨询来源的数量而滑动。这种滑动随着读者可靠性的增加而增大。当来源可靠性不同时，投票规则和后验排序实例的方式不同。

    arXiv:2608.14509v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Systems that ask a language model to reach a conclusion from many sources usually concatenate them into one prompt. This conflates two operations with different requirements. Interpreting a source rewards capacity and context. Combining interpretations rewards fixed arithmetic, comparability across instances, and the option to return nothing. Once separated, the design problem becomes the interface between them. We propose a four-field evidence tuple (hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance) and show that fixing it determines both halves. The separation also reveals a failure mode in how such systems combine, which we call count-scale drift. Thresholding a sum of unnormalized weights is exactly posterior thresholding, but at an operating point that slides with the number of sources consulted. The slide grows with reader reliability. When source reliabilities differ, the vote rule and the posterior order instances differentl
    
[^3]: RecipeNet：用于配方数据的层次化Transformer模型

    RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)

    RecipeNet是一种层次化Transformer架构，通过编码步骤内字段交互和跨步骤顺序依赖，在配方数据表示学习上显著优于现有表格模型。

    

    arXiv:2608.14505v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：配方数据出现在材料合成、药物配方和工业制造等领域，其中过程被表示为包含异构结构化字段的有序步骤序列。现有的表格学习方法通常将这些结构展平为固定模式表示，限制了其捕捉层次化字段交互和过程依赖的能力。我们提出了RecipeNet，一种层次化Transformer架构，通过堆叠的Transformer编码器编码每个步骤内的字段级交互以及跨步骤的顺序依赖。在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet持续优于现有表格模型，突显了层次化和顺序建模对配方表示学习的价值。

    arXiv:2608.14505v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.
    
[^4]: 晶体材料的通用热力学原子间势

    Universal Thermodynamic Interatomic Potentials for Crystalline Materials

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14502)

    本文提出了一种名为热力学原子间势（TIP）的新型模型，将原子间势扩展为热力学一致的吉布斯自由能模型，通过自动微分和通用势UMA实现，能高效计算晶体自由能并预测相变，从而加速材料发现。

    

    自由能决定了固态相的稳定性，然而计算材料发现仍主要依赖于基态能量，因为自由能计算需要系综平均。我们引入了热力学原子间势（TIP），它将原子间势从静态能量扩展到热力学一致的吉布斯自由能模型，通过自动微分从温度和压力推导出热力学响应。我们使用通用势UMA实现了TIP[UMA]，在从准谐近似到分子动力学精度的自由能数据上训练，并将其校准到更高分辨率的计算或实验。通过单次评估，它返回晶体的状态方程，并定位竞争分支之间的相变，包括动态稳定相。微调将模型扩展到合金溶解度极限和混溶间隙。TIP使自由能计算变得简便高效。

    arXiv:2608.14502v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Free energies govern solid-state phase stability, yet computational materials discovery still relies largely on ground-state energies because free energy calculations require ensemble averages. We introduce the thermodynamic interatomic potential (TIP), which extends an interatomic potential from its static energy to a thermodynamically consistent Gibbs free energy model, with thermodynamic responses following from temperature and pressure by automatic differentiation. We implement TIP[UMA] using the universal potential UMA, train it on free energies from quasi-harmonic to molecular dynamics fidelity, and calibrate it to higher-resolution calculations or experiment. From a single evaluation, it returns the equation of state of a crystal and locates phase transitions among competing branches, including dynamically stabilized phases. Fine-tuning extends the model to alloy solubility limits and miscibility gaps. TIP makes the free energy 
    
[^5]: Rollplex：面向视觉语言模型后训练的跨阶段GPU空间共享

    Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)

    Rollplex通过将VLM后训练中的前缀计算与回放解码并行化，打破传统串行阶段限制，提升GPU利用率且不破坏同步同策略语义。

    

    视觉语言模型（VLMs）使具身智能体能够根据视觉观察和语言指令进行推理和行动。强化学习（RL）后训练利用任务反馈增强这些能力，但当前的同策略RL运行时在严格的串行阶段中执行回放、参考评分和演员训练。虽然这种方法对纯文本RL有效，但对VLMs而言，这种阶段粒度的执行是浪费的，因为处理密集视频输入和提示前缀占据了每个阶段的大部分时间。由于前缀处理独立于生成的响应，它可以与回放解码并行运行，这会使GPU计算能力利用不足，同时不破坏同步同策略语义。我们提出了Rollplex，一个将参考和训练阶段分解并将前缀计算移入回放解码窗口的运行时。实现这种调度需要超越并发内核启动：简单的共存...

    arXiv:2608.14498v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408)

    本文为分布时序差分学习提供了在线推断的理论框架，证明了平均估计器和自助法的渐近正态性，并扩展到了非光滑泛函的局部推断。

    

    我们研究了在固定策略下，针对回报分布泛函的在线统计推断问题。回报分布通过非参数分布时序差分学习从单一马尔可夫轨迹中估计。对于Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器，我们证明了其根号T误差在Cramér空间中弱收敛于中心高斯随机元素。我们还证明了，在观测轨迹的条件下，自助法与原始平均值之间的根号T差异弱收敛于相同的高斯极限。这些结果证明了对于光滑统计泛函（包括方差、CVaR、期望短缺和期望分位数）的自助推断是合理的。对于非光滑统计泛函，我们开发了在有限多个阈值的$T^{-1/2}$邻域内估计回报CDF的局部渐近理论，以及其自助对应版本。该理论使我们能够进行推断。

The return distribution is estimated by nonparametric distributional temporal-difference learning from a single Markov trajectory. For the Polyak--Ruppert averaged estimator, we prove that its root-$T$ error converges weakly to a centered Gaussian random element in Cram\'er space. We also prove that, conditionally on the observed trajectory, the root-$T$ difference between the bootstrap and original averages converges weakly to the same Gaussian limit. These results justify bootstrap inference for smooth statistical functionals, including variance, CVaR, expected shortfall, and expectiles. For nonsmooth statistical functionals, we develop a local asymptotic theory for the estimated return CDF over $T^{-1/2}$-neighborhoods of finitely many thresholds, together with its bootstrap analogue. [^17]: 基于扩散模型的离线深度Q*估计

    Offline Deep Q* Estimation with Diffusion Models

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401)

    本文提出一种新框架，利用扩散模型估计奖励和转移核以近似最优贝尔曼算子，并通过解耦算子估计与价值学习，在理论上证明了非渐近收敛速率。

    

    在离线强化学习中，估计最优动作价值函数Q*可以表述为仅基于离线观测求解最优贝尔曼方程。一个基本挑战是奖励函数和转移核未知，因此最优贝尔曼算子无法直接从数据中观测到。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖框架，将算子估计与价值函数学习解耦。在该方法中，我们首先构建条件扩散模型来估计奖励规律和转移核，这引出了一个数据驱动的最优贝尔曼算子近似。然后，我们将这些估计器代入贝尔曼方程，并通过在神经网络函数类上最小化经验贝尔曼残差来获得Q*的深度估计器。理论上，我们首先通过端到端分析建立了学习最优贝尔曼算子的尖锐非渐近收敛速率。

    arXiv:2608.14401v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: In offline RL, estimating the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ can be formulated as solving the optimal Bellman equation based solely on offline observations. A fundamental challenge is that the reward function and transition kernel are unknown, so the optimal Bellman operator is not directly observable from data. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that decouples operator estimation from value function learning. In this approach, we first formulate conditional diffusion models to estimate the reward law and transition kernel, which induces a data-driven approximation of the optimal Bellman operator. We then plug these estimators into the Bellman equation and obtain a deep estimator of $Q^*$ by minimizing the empirical Bellman residual over a neural network function class. Theoretically, we first establish sharp nonasymptotic convergence rates for learning the optimal Bellman operator through an end-to-end analysis o

    混合专家（MoE）模型已被广泛应用于实时交互应用，如编程助手、实时音视频交互系统。为了满足这些场景中极低的响应延迟要求，实践者通常采用小批量解码，此时MoE推理变得内存受限，并严重受限于专家权重加载的瓶颈。然而，这一瓶颈受到的关注有限，现有的解决方案，如训练后权重压缩或预训练期间的细粒度专家设计，要么降低模型精度，要么引入额外的计算和通信开销。为解决此问题，我们提出了DeaMoE，一种解码高效的MoE架构，其中专家被分组为多个部门，属于同一部门的专家由于来自同一专业领域而共享大部分参数，此外每个专家还具有额外的特有参数。

However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. [^19]: 错误但有用：多智能体消息中超越答案正确性的轨迹价值

    多智能体推理系统通常使用一致性、置信度或自动评分来决定哪些消息应影响最终答案。这种过滤假设了一条可能正确的消息也值得保留。然而，一个错误的答案可能包含有用的分解、约束或科学原理。我们通过多样假设审议（DHD）来检验这一区别，这是一种受控测量协议，它缓存五个独立生成的消息，并重放相同的下游求解器（称为整合器），每次提供或隐藏一条消息。重放比较衡量消息的轨迹价值：即让该消息可用是否有助于或有害于后续推理。在五个数学和科学基准测试以及两个公开可用的模型家族（gpt-oss-120b和gemma-4-31B-it）中，错误但有帮助的消息出现在每个基准-模型组合中。在改变最终正确性的错误答案消息中，大多数情况下...

    arXiv:2608.14375v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning systems often use agreement, confidence, or automated scores to decide which messages should shape a final answer. Such filtering assumes that a message likely to be correct is also worth keeping. Yet a wrong answer can contain a useful decomposition, constraint, or scientific principle. We test this distinction with Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation (DHD), a controlled measurement protocol that caches five independently generated messages and replays the same downstream solver, called the integrator, with each message available or hidden. Across five mathematics and science benchmarks and two openly available model families, gpt-oss-120b and gemma-4-31B-it, wrong-helpful messages appear in every benchmark-model combination.     本研究证明随机权重平均在增强数据上能提供超出其单独性能提升的等变性增强，且无需重复训练成本。

    

    arXiv:2608.14373v1 公告类型：新论文 摘要：学习任务的对称性已成为设计现代深度学习解决方案的重要因素。数据增强是一种将对称性融入通用神经网络的直接且有效的方法。最近的结果表明，在增强数据上训练时，无限大的深度集成表现出完美的对称性。然而，由于训练集成需要多次重复训练过程，这种方法成本高昂。在本研究中，我们研究了随机权重平均（SWA）作为一种替代性集成技术，它不需要重复训练运行。我们通过用奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克过程近似训练结束时的随机训练轨迹来分析SWA。我们表明，在无限宽度极限下，对增强数据应用SWA提供了超越仅由SWA带来的性能提升所预期的等变性增强。我们通过广泛的数值实验验证了我们的结果。

    arXiv:2608.14373v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical expe

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)

    本文提出了一种名为“imposter”的自监督预文本任务，通过跨实体特征置换来捕捉物理一致性，并在ERA5-Land数据上验证了其在多项下游任务中的有效性，首次系统比较了地表建模中的SSL目标。

    

    arXiv:2608.14372v1 公告类型：新 摘要：科学数据通常描述由物理定律共同支配其特征的实体，但现有的自监督学习（SSL）目标在很大程度上忽略了这种物理一致性。我们引入了“imposter”这一判别性预文本任务，该任务将实体的特征子集替换为另一个实体捐赠的真实观测值，并训练编码器识别被替换的特征。由于每个捐赠值在个体上都是合理的，该任务只能通过学习跨特征的物理依赖性来解决。我们在全球ERA5-Land再分析数据上，使用21个环境变量评估了所提出的目标，并在涵盖气候分类、碳通量估计和河流流量预测的七项下游任务上评估了学习到的表示。我们的研究包括，据我们所知，在共享架构和预训练设置下，对地表建模自监督目标的首次系统比较。

    arXiv:2608.14372v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. [^24]: ATLAS：通过LLM引导的抽象与自动机学习发现智能体策略

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352)

    ATLAS通过结合轨迹抽象和自动机学习，从智能体行为轨迹中提取可解释的有限状态模型，以揭示其策略和模式。

ATLAS combines trace abstraction with automata learning to infer finite-state models that capture observed agent-environment interaction strategies. These models provide human-interpretable insights and support automated analyses of recurring behaviors, decision points, successful task-completion paths, and failure loops.     本文提出一种零参数、无需训练的轨迹预测方法，通过状态条件转移采样和核检索，在数据稀缺时性能优于大型Transformer模型，并能用极少历史数据适应新区域。

    

    arXiv:2608.14349v1 公告类型：新 摘要：我们提出了一种无需训练的轨迹预测方法，用于多模态轨迹预测，其精度可与一个5700万参数的Transformer模型相媲美，但无需GPU且零学习参数。该方法构建了一个历史状态到下一位置对的转移表，并使用基于空间邻近性、方位、速度和时间上下文的乘积核来检索邻居。两种推理模式在此共享表示上运行：多样性惩罚采样生成覆盖不同合理路线的轨迹，而束搜索则找到最高似然路径。在TrAISformer基准测试（丹麦海事AIS）上，我们的方法在完整数据可用性下达到了竞争性精度，并在数据稀缺情况下显著优于Transformer——在训练数据降至10%时仍保持稳定，而TrAISformer在此情况下性能急剧退化。这使得该方法能够从少一个数量级的历史数据中部署到新的地理区域。

The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical
    
[^26]: 拨开迷雾：为LLM智能体安装并优化主动探索能力

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14339)

    本论文提出了一种名为\ours的方法，通过探索性数据构建和对比信号引导的强化学习，有效增强了LLM智能体的主动探索能力，克服了后见之明偏差并区分有效探索与冗余行为。

    

    arXiv:2608.14339v1 公告类型：新 摘要：我们研究了LLM智能体中的主动探索，即探索环境以获取信息从而改善未来决策的能力。在这方面，我们首先识别出阻碍这一能力的两个基本瓶颈，然后提出了一种名为\ours的新方法，旨在灌输并优化主动探索。具体来说，\ours包括两个组成部分：（1）探索性数据构建，通过合成富含探索的轨迹来缓解标准演示中的后见之明偏差；（2）带对比信号引导的强化学习优化，利用对比轨迹对来区分有效探索与冗余徘徊。大量实验证明了\ours的有效性，并提供了对主动探索特征的深入见解。我们的代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/GuanZhizhao/SAFARI。

    arXiv:2608.14339v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: We study proactive exploration in LLM agents, i.e., the ability to explore an environment to acquire information that improves future decision-making. In this regard, we first identify two fundamental bottlenecks that hinder this capability and then propose \ours, a novel method designed to instill and refine proactive exploration. Specifically, \ours\ consists of two components: (1) Exploratory Data Construction, which synthesizes exploration-rich trajectories to mitigate the hindsight bias of standard demonstrations; and (2) RL Optimization with Contrastive Signal Guidance, which leverages contrastive trajectory pairs to distinguish productive exploration from redundant wandering. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of \ours\ and provide insights into the characteristics of proactive exploration. Our code is available at: https://github.com/GuanZhizhao/SAFARI.

    arXiv:2608.14332v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：强化学习在自主城市驾驶中具有前景，但长时程目标导向导航要求策略同时习得多种竞争性行为——到达远处目标、跟踪路线、避开障碍物、遵守信号——而固定的目标函数无法规定学习这些行为的顺序。本文提出CORAL，它同时推进两个调度机制：一个五阶段课程，逐步延长路线并收紧行为约束；以及一个阶段感知奖励，其组件权重随任务难度增加，从任务进展转向路线跟随、安全性、平滑性和规则合规性。该策略是一个多流Actor-Critic网络，在CARLA中使用近端策略优化（PPO）训练，基于紧凑的99维状态，该状态结合了极坐标LiDAR直方图与车辆遥测、自车框架路线几何和交通规则指示——无需点云编码器，无需鸟瞰视图栅格化。

    arXiv:2608.14332v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Reinforcement learning is promising for autonomous urban driving, but long-horizon goal-directed navigation asks a policy to acquire several competing behaviors at once--reaching a distant goal, tracking a route, avoiding obstacles, obeying signals--and a fixed objective gives no order in which to learn them. This paper presents CORAL, which advances two schedules together: a five-stage curriculum that progressively lengthens routes and tightens behavioral constraints, and a stage-aware reward whose component weights shift emphasis from mission progress toward route following, safety, smoothness, and rule compliance as the task hardens. The policy is a multi-stream actor-critic network trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in CARLA on a compact 99-dimensional state pairing a polar LiDAR histogram with vehicle telemetry, ego-frame route geometry, and traffic-rule indicators--no point-cloud encoder, no bird's-eye-view rasteriza

    本文首次证明了量子多臂老虎机和有限动作量子线性老虎机的极小极大遗憾下界，解决了是否存在与时间范围无关的遗憾问题，并提供了基于多项式方法和Remez型不等式的新证明技术。

    

    arXiv:2608.14319v1 公告类型：新 摘要：我们在Wan等人[2023]的模型下研究量子多臂老虎机（QMAB）和量子线性老虎机（QLB），其中学习者通过量子奖励预言机或其逆来查询每个臂或动作。先前的工作给出了在时间范围$T$上的算法，对于具有$K$个臂的QMAB，遗憾为$O(K\log T)$，对于$d$维的QLB，遗憾为$O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$。这留下了$K\log T$规模是否不可避免以及$d^2$依赖是否可以改进的问题。我们证明了第一个极小极大下界，对于QMAB为$\Omega(K\log(T/K))$，对于有限动作QLB为$\Omega(d\log(T/d))$，解决了Wan等人[2023]提出的关于是否存在与$T$无关的遗憾的问题。我们论证的核心是一个高置信度的单臂量子测试下界，用于区分固定的奖励均值与一个替代区间，通过多项式方法和三角多项式的Remez型不等式证明。

[2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $\Omega(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $\Omega(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. [^31]: 通过影响函数检测受污染的代码生成提示批次

    Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)

    提出了一种基于影响函数的威胁模型无关检测方法CodeSIFT，能有效识别代码生成提示批次中的污染，在中高注入率下AUROC高达0.98。

    

    大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于代码生成，但它们仍然容易受到引发不安全实现的提示的影响。现有的防御措施通常依赖于预定义威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，限制了它们对新型攻击的有效性。我们提出了CodeSIFT，一种不依赖特定威胁模型的检测方法，利用影响函数来识别导致模型行为异常的提示批次。CodeSIFT不检测特定漏洞，而是衡量生成代码在参数空间中的影响，并使用统计测试来确定候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布。为了评估我们的方法，我们引入了两个覆盖多种漏洞的基准数据集。我们在三个参数范围从3B到7B的开源代码LLM上评估了CodeSIFT，在中高注入率下实现了高达0.98的AUROC分数。

    arXiv:2608.14303v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rate

    高内涵显微镜技术能够系统性地分析细胞对化学扰动的响应，但化学空间的规模使得全面的表型表征在实验上不可行。这促使我们开发计算模型，无需获取相应的处理细胞即可预测图像衍生的表型。我们将分子诱导的表型预测问题表述为图像表示空间中的归纳条件传输问题。给定阴性对照表型和分子结构，我们旨在预测该分子诱导的表型。我们首先评估经典最优传输基线方法，并表明静态耦合在大规模表型图像数据集上无法产生有效预测。随后，我们引入一个分子条件化的神经最优传输（NOT）模型，该模型采用Monge-Gap正则化训练目标，学习将未扰动的阴性对照传输到目标状态。

We formulate molecule-induced phenotype prediction as an inductive conditional transport problem in image representation space. Given a negative-control phenotype and the structure of a molecule, we aim to predict the phenotype induced by the corresponding molecule. We first evaluate classical optimal transport baselines and show that static couplings do not yield useful predictions on large-scale phenotypic image datasets. We then introduce a molecule-conditioned Neural Optimal Transport (NOT) model with a Monge-Gap regularization training objective that learns to transport negative-control unperturbed

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)

    本文提出了多种新的凸损失函数用于SVM和神经网络，证明了它们是标准损失的推广，但实验结果显示这些新损失并未显著提升泛化性能。

    

    arXiv:2608.14288v1 公告类型：新 摘要：我们提出了多种用于SVM和神经网络的新凸损失函数，应用于二分类任务。尽管在利用对偶SVM模型时存在实际限制，但我们能够在SVM原始形式和神经网络中使用它们。具体来说，使用粒子群优化算法解决了带有修改损失函数的原始SVM问题。我们证明了所提出的损失函数是标准损失函数的推广，并在几个小型数据集上进行了实验。这项初步研究表明，在损失函数中利用模式相关性理论上可以增强某些数据集的泛化性能。为了评估每种损失函数的性能，我们采用了嵌套交叉验证程序。结果显示，使用或不使用新损失函数，泛化度量是相同的。

    arXiv:2608.14288v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations   inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

    Pairton提出了一种基于图结构和迭代掩码预测的通用粒子重建框架，通过动态成对表示实现全局一致性，并在全强子t\bar{t}衰变中达到最先进性能。

    

    arXiv:2608.14278v1 公告类型：交叉 摘要：我们提出了Pairton，一个用于高能碰撞事件中短寿命粒子重建的迭代框架。通过将粒子重建表述为图结构上的掩码预测过程，Pairton学习与衰变产物因子分解一致的条件分布，并迭代预测表示粒子衰变关系的邻接矩阵中的边。利用基于pairformer架构并动态更新成对表示的方法，我们的框架融入了全局事件一致性。我们在全强子$t\bar{t}$衰变上展示了最先进的性能。Pairton为粒子重建提供了一种通用、灵活的范式，并可轻松扩展到其他拓扑结构，连接了现代生成建模和高能物理的思想。

    arXiv:2608.14278v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: We present Pairton, an iterative framework for reconstructing short-lived particles in high-energy collision events. By formulating particle reconstruction as a masked prediction process over graph structures, Pairton learns conditional distributions consistent with a factorised decomposition of decay products and iteratively predicts edges in the adjacency matrix representing particle decay relationships. Leveraging a pairformer-based architecture with dynamically updated pairwise representations, our method incorporates global event consistency. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on fully hadronic $t\bar{t}$ decays. Pairton provides a general, flexible paradigm for particle reconstruction and can be readily extended to other topologies, bridging ideas from modern generative modelling and high-energy physics.

We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. [^36]: 气象驱动的垃圾填埋场逸散排放因果即时预报助力公共卫生主动响应

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14254](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14254)

    该论文提出CAIRN框架，利用常规气象数据直接识别垃圾填埋场逸散排放的气象驱动因素，实现主动而非被动的公共卫生响应。

    

    废物场所的逸散排放日益使社区暴露于有毒和恶臭气体中，然而公共卫生响应大多仍为回顾性的，只有在居民暴露后才对事件进行调查。在此，我们表明，在一个长期监测的欧洲垃圾填埋场中，硫化氢（HS）升高的气象驱动因素及其作用的时间尺度，可以直接从常规监测数据中识别出来。我们引入了CAIRN（受体即时预报的因果锚定推断），这是一个机器学习框架，其内部记忆与这些测量到的时间尺度相匹配：一个快速组件跟踪小时尺度的风载传输，一个慢速组件跟踪多小时的天气变化。CAIRN训练用于预测气体测量值，仅使用常规气象变量和日历，无需手工设计的特征。其行为与识别的传输机制一致，且该框架可追踪...

    arXiv:2608.14254v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Fugitive emissions from waste sites increasingly expose communities to toxic and odorous gases, yet public-health responses remain largely retrospective, with episodes investigated only after residents have been exposed. Here we show that the meteorological drivers of elevated hydrogen sulphide (HS) at a long-monitored European landfill, and the timescales over which they act, can be identified directly from routine monitoring data. We introduce CAIRN (Causal-Anchored Inference for Receptor Nowcasting), a machine-learning framework whose internal memory is matched to these measured timescales: a fast component tracking hour-scale wind-borne transport and a slow component tracking multi-hour weather changes. Trained to predict gas measurements, CAIRN operates using only routine weather variables and the calendar, without hand-engineered features. It is usually measured by training a classifier under one preprocessing pipeline and testing it under another, with preserved accuracy read as evidence of learning. We revisit that reading, using a Raman foundation model as a case study. Such models normalize their inputs before any learned parameter is applied. If that normalization maps two differently preprocessed spectra to the same vector, the encoder receives identical inputs, so the invariance cannot be attributed to learning. For a normalization that uses each spectrum's own statistics, this happens exactly when one spectrum is a positive multiple of the other plus a constant. In this work, we incorporate fairness constraints directly into TFM training, enabling fair predictions in a single forward pass.     本文提出了一种基于特征重要性度量的自适应保护机制，在符号回归进化过程中选择性保留重要构造特征，从而防止有效特征丢失并提升信用分类性能。

    

    进化特征构造在符号回归中展现出强大潜力，它通过自动发现输入特征的信息性变换来增强简单基学习器的性能。然而，现有方法通常缺乏显式机制来保留进化过程中发现的重要构造特征，当遗传算子破坏有效特征时，宝贵的遗传物质可能会丢失。本文引入一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性度量在进化过程中选择性保留构造特征。该机制对更重要的构造特征提供更强的保护，同时允许较不重要的特征被修改，并从更重要的特征中整合有用的构建块。我们使用多种特征重要性计算方法评估该方法，并证明其在不同基学习器上的鲁棒性。实验结果...

This paper introduces an adaptive protection mechanism that leverages feature importance metrics to selectively preserve constructed features during evolution. The mechanism provides stronger protection for more important constructed features while still allowing less important features to be modified and to incorporate useful building blocks from more important features. Existing approaches rely on routing statistics collected after each MoE router, requiring expert weight load or migration to begin only after routing decisions are available, consequently placing migration overhead on the inference critical path. In this work, we observe that online balancing can instead be largely overlapped with computation before target routing (e.g., attention), if routing distributions can be predicted accurately in advance.     [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14191](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14191)

    本文提出注意力感知变换编码（AATC），通过将KV缓存压缩问题建模为变换编码与反向注水，在考虑注意力误差传播的情况下优化比特分配，从而显著提升长上下文推理的内存效率。

    

    键值（KV）缓存存储了来自过去令牌的信息，是长上下文推理中的主要内存瓶颈。现有的量化方法通过用较低精度的数据类型统一表示KV缓存，并设计量化方案以最小化缓存本身的重建误差来解决这一瓶颈，但未考虑该误差如何通过注意力机制传播。我们证明，在白噪声量化模型下，预期的注意力感知失真可分解为可加的键和值贡献，这些贡献在令牌和通道上分解。基于变换编码和反向注水（信号处理和率失真理论中的经典工具），我们引入了注意力感知变换编码（AATC），该编码在标定集上分配比特以最小化注意力感知失真。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct上，在LongBenc上进行评估。

    arXiv:2608.14191v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache stores information from past tokens and is a major memory bottleneck in long-context inference. Existing quantization methods address this bottleneck by representing the KV cache uniformly with lower-precision data types and designing quantization schemes to minimize reconstruction error in the cache itself, without accounting for how that error propagates through attention mechanisms. We prove that, under a white-noise quantization model, the expected attention-aware distortion decomposes into additive key and value contributions that factor across tokens and channels. Building on transform coding and reverse water-filling, which are classical tools from signal processing and rate-distortion theory, we introduce Attention-Aware Transform Coding (AATC), which allocates bits over a calibration set to minimize attention-aware distortion. We evaluate a two-hidden-layer DBM on two tabular benchmarks spanning distinct domains (UCI Bank Marketing and NSL-KDD) against eight classical and modern baselines across twenty random seeds.     [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14177)

    本文提出SGSAN模型，通过显式学习静态有向依赖图来增强交通流预测的可解释性，克服传统模型缺乏透明性的问题。

We obtained a robust model that gives a fast optimal solution for problems of small and medium size, and gives fast suboptimal solution for problems of larger (> 200) size.

    随着全球城市人口的增长，具有现实约束的路线优化问题变得极为重要。虽然我们知道理论上能提供精确最优解的方法，但随着问题规模增大，由于指数级复杂度，其应用变得困难。我们研究了带时间窗的容量约束车辆路径问题（CVRPTW），并比较了精确求解器SCIP与启发式算法（如LKH、2-OPT、3-OPT、ORTools框架）以及深度学习模型JAMPR所得的解。我们证明，对于规模为50的问题，深度学习和经典启发式解接近SCIP的精确解，但所需时间更少。此外，对于规模为100的问题，SCIP精确方法比神经和经典启发式方法慢约13倍，但路径成本相同，而在相同时间内首次可行解的质量差约50%。

Additionally for problems with size 100, SCIP exact methods around 13 times slower that neural and classical heuristics with the same route cost and on around 50% worse for the first feasible solution on the same time.     本文提出了AgilePE，一个通过自我对弈强化学习实现自主无人机追逃的完整系统，该框架将敏捷控制、竞争性策略优化和仿真到现实部署统一起来，实现端到端的敏捷机动。

    

    自主追逃是无人机（UAV）面临的一项基本挑战，需要在紧密耦合的动态环境和不断变化的对手行为下做出快速决策。传统的基于规则或微分博弈的方法通常难以应对高维空中交互和敏捷机动。我们提出了AgilePE，一个通过自我对弈强化学习实现自主无人机追逃的完整系统。AgilePE在一个统一框架中集成了敏捷低层控制、竞争性策略优化和仿真到现实部署。该策略直接将机载状态观测映射到集体推力和机体速率（CTBR）命令，无需中间轨迹规划器或航点控制器即可实现端到端的敏捷机动。在训练方面，我们使用带有优先级虚构自我对弈（PFSP）和多样化对手池的竞争性自我对弈，使智能体能够针对历史对手不断改进。

Traditional rule-based or differential-game approaches often struggle with high-dimensional aerial interactions and agile maneuvering. We present AgilePE, a complete system for autonomous UAV pursuit-evasion via self-play reinforcement learning. AgilePE integrates agile low-level control, competitive policy optimization, and sim-to-real deployment in a unified framework. The policy directly maps onboard state observations to Collective Thrust and Body Rates (CTBR) commands, enabling end-to-end agile maneuvering without intermediate trajectory planners or waypoint controllers. [^54]: 通过主动解释引导克服图神经网络中的捷径学习

    [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14121](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14121)

    XIGL是一种人机回环主动解释引导方法，通过检测并纠正图神经网络中的捷径学习，提升模型在分布外任务中的可靠性。

    

    图神经网络（GNNs）可能通过无意中利用捷径（即与预测相关但非因果的边、节点和特征）来解决预测任务，这损害了它们在分布外任务中的可靠性。我们引入了XIGL，一种与架构无关的人机回环策略，用于从GNNs中移除此类捷径。我们的关键见解有两个方面。一方面，通过检查GNN解释可以检测对捷径的依赖。另一方面，一旦用户意识到此类捷径，足够专业的用户可以提供量身定制的纠正性反馈，这有助于消除模型的混淆。XIGL支持任何查询策略；然而，由于获取纠正性反馈可能成本高昂，我们开发了一种主动学习策略，用于优先处理更可能展示捷径行为的解释，从而降低标注和认知成本。我们展示了XIGL的有效性，包括b（截断部分）。

    arXiv:2608.14121v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) can solve prediction tasks by unintentionally exploiting shortcuts---that is, edges, nodes, and features that correlate with but are not causal for the prediction---which compromise their reliability in out-of-distribution tasks. We introduce XIGL, an architecture-agnostic human-in-the-loop strategy for removing such shortcuts from GNNs. Our key insight is twofold. On the one hand, reliance on shortcuts can be detected by inspecting GNN explanations. On the other hand, once made aware of such shortcuts, sufficiently expert users can provide tailored corrective feedback, which helps deconfound the model. XIGL supports any query strategy; however, since corrective feedback can be expensive to acquire, we develop an active learning strategy for prioritizing explanations that are more likely to display shortcut behavior, lowering annotation and cognitive costs. We showcase the effectiveness of XIGL, including b

    本文提出一种固定预算下的结构感知高斯体编码方法，通过解析分配基元并直接细化，实现了高效的体数据压缩，显著降低编码时间。

    

    科学模拟通常产生的标量体数据速度超过其存储、传输和加载能力，而原位缩减只能使用模拟资源中有限的一部分。本工作将标量场编码为各向异性高斯基元，在固定预算下进行。完整的基元集从局部场结构中解析分配，包括位置、方向和形状，然后直接针对标量场进行细化，无需增密、剪枝或改变数量。所选预算在细化前确定编码存储，并与迭代计划一起，提供可控的细化时间预算。在受控基准测试中，截断感知的场评估将编码时间减少了高达51倍；140万个高斯在单个桌面GPU上最多四分钟内编码十亿体素体积，减少迭代的细化在一分钟内完成。跨越五个数据集（涵盖2.1米范围）的测试中，该方法表现稳定。

The selected budget determines encoded storage before refinement and, together with the iteration schedule, provides a controllable refinement-time budget. In a controlled benchmark, truncation-aware field evaluation reduces encoding time by up to 51x; 1.4 million Gaussians encode a billion-voxel volume in at most four minutes on one desktop GPU, with reduced-iteration refinement completing in under one minute. We identify two distinct reasons behind it: low predictability limits the amplitude of calibrated point forecasts, while per-series objectives leave cross-series structure unidentified. The cost function we consider captures the complexity of predicting the outcome generated by the environment, in a scenario where the aforementioned prediction is performed via comparative queries to a lying oracle. We consider both stochastic and adversarial environments, propose algorithms for both settings, and establish logarithmic upper bounds on their regret.

In this paper, we learn the guidance schedule as a function of diffusion time, conditioning and the current noisy sample, in order to better align sampled images with the text prompt. We frame this as a density ratio estimation problem: a discriminator is trained to estimate the time-dependent log-density ratio between the true and guided marginal distributions, while a lightweight generator network predicts the optimal, state-dependent guidance scale.     [https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14020)

    本文提出“插入稳定性”概念，揭示并非所有学习器都会因额外正确数据而受损，只有非插入稳定的学习器才会面临对数因子代价，从而填补了维度理论在最坏情况分析中的空白。

    

    arXiv:2608.14020v1 公告类型：新公告 摘要：添加已知正确的数据本应是安全的，但并非总是如此。Larsen、Pabbaraju和Shetty用单调对手模型化了这一失败现象，该对手读取独立同分布的训练样本，并可以随意附加更多示例，前提是目标假设对所有示例都进行标注。Mehrotra随后解决了成本问题，表明对于VC维度d >= 2的类别，任何学习器都无法保证期望误差优于Theta((d/n)log(en/d))，这比干净的PAC率高出一个对数因子。由于该率是所有类别的最坏情况，它并未说明哪些类别实际上会遭受惩罚，而答案取决于学习器。我们称一个学习器为插入稳定的，如果向其提供更多正确标注的示例只会缩小其出错的区域。此类学习器对对手免疫，因为在任何给定样本上，插入后的风险永远不会超过仅干净部分的风险，无论添加多少数据以及如何巧妙地添加。

    arXiv:2608.14020v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Adding data known to be correct ought to be safe. Not always. Larsen, Pabbaraju and Shetty model the failure with a monotone adversary, which reads an i.i.d. training sample and may append as many further examples as it likes, provided the target hypothesis labels them all. Mehrotra has since settled the cost, showing that for classes of VC dimension d >= 2 no learner can guarantee expected error better than Theta((d/n)log(en/d)), a logarithmic factor above the clean PAC rate.   Because that rate is a worst case over all classes, it says nothing about which classes actually suffer the penalty, and the answer turns on the learner. We call a learner insertion-stable if feeding it more correctly labeled examples can only shrink the region where it errs. Such learners are immune to the adversary, since on any given sample the risk after insertions never exceeds the risk on the clean part alone, however much is added and however cleverly it i

    本文提出涌现模型（EMs），一种基于简单动态基底（如元胞自动机）的机器学习范式，通过进化搜索训练，并证明其具有潜在通用性，能实现任何部分可计算函数，且偏向全局泛化。

Such substrates typically iterate a fixed local rule over a latent space for an adaptive number of steps, with an interface linking the latent state to external input/output signals. Training proceeds by evolutionary search. We hypothesize that some instances of this framework are biased toward global generalization: capturing the rule generating the data over its full domain, and therefore extrapolating beyond the training range. A version-space semantics makes background knowledge and open- versus closed-world assumptions explicit. For finite open-world prompts with positive and negative comparisons, we prove an exact completion trichotomy: after taking the reflexive transitive closure of the positive demonstrations, a query is forced true, forced false because every true completion creates a cycle or violates a negative demonstration, or remains genuinely ambiguous. For a known $n$-element universe, we characterize the open-world teaching

    本文提出QUASAR，一种在量化感知训练过程中持续进行轻量级损失感知重构的方法，以降低损失下限并提升低比特模型质量。

This mismatch can lead to suboptimal training trajectories and a higher loss floor. Second-order PTQ methods mitigate a similar gap by minimizing loss-aware reconstruction error, but doing it once for a frozen model can take hours; repeating this process throughout QAT as the weights evolve is impractical.     联邦学习（FL）使分布式设备能够在不共享原始数据的情况下协作训练模型；然而，在实际中它面临显著的通信瓶颈和信道损伤。传统的网络层处理方法要么将信道理想化为无差错，要么对传输的模型更新应用等错误保护（EEP），未能考虑到单个本地模型中量化比特固有的重要性差异。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种基于极化码的跨层联邦学习方案，利用极化码在有限块长度下的不等错误保护（UEP）特性。具体而言，该设计选择性地保护更重要的量化比特，从而减轻信道噪声的有害影响。我们进一步对所提方案进行了严格的收敛性分析，推导出收敛差距的上界，并随后对其进行联合优化。

    arXiv:2608.13961v1 Announce Type: new  Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data; however, it faces significant communication bottlenecks and channel impairments in practice. To address this limitation, we propose a cross layer polar code based FL scheme that leverages the unequal error protection (UEP) property of polar codes under finite block lengths. Specifically, the proposed design selectively protects more significant quantization bits, thereby mitigating the detrimental effects of channel noise. [^78]: 面向隐私感知联邦生物信号学习的混合量子启发柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络

These constraints require classifiers that are both communication-efficient and robust to cross-client distribution shifts.     机器学习（ML）已成为现代工程设计工作流程中不可或缺的一部分。训练ML模型的关键步骤之一是选择损失函数，这可以通过最大似然估计（MLE）和交叉验证等多种技术系统化地制定。尽管MLE是训练ML模型最流行、有效且直观的机制之一，但它具有脆弱性：如果支撑其的假设不成立，训练出的ML模型可能泛化能力较差。这种脆弱性甚至影响到高斯过程（GPs），而GPs在工程设计中广泛使用，且常被（错误地）认为对过拟合非常稳健。在本文中，我们从根本上评估了MLE在训练GPs用于概率回归或分类任务时的脆弱性。我们将理论上有依据的指标与MLE进行比较，并提出实用解决方案。我们的广泛研究表明...

A crucial step in training an ML model is the selection of the loss function which can be systematically formulated via various techniques such as maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and cross-validation . While MLE is one of the most popular, effective, and intuitive mechanisms for training ML models, it is brittle: if the assumptions underpinning it are not met, the trained ML model may generalize poorly. This brittleness affects even Gaussian processes (GPs) which are widely used in engineering design and are often (incorrectly) presumed to be very robust to overfitting.     CutClean通过在每个网络块添加辅助隐私头量化信息泄露，并应用递增稀疏性剪枝，在保持高稀疏性的同时显著减少隐私属性泄露。

    

    神经网络越来越多地部署在高风险应用中，隐私泄露问题日益严重。我们表明，即使在不存在导致传统数据集偏差的表征不平衡的情况下，这种隐私泄露也可能发生。这在部署处理敏感属性的模型时构成了显著的隐私风险。在此背景下，我们提出了CutClean，一种隐私感知的剪枝方法，它允许减少通过网络的信息流中的隐私内容，同时增加其稀疏性。我们的方法在每个网络块处放置辅助线性隐私头来量化信息泄露，并进一步应用递增的稀疏性水平以移除私有属性泄露，该泄露通过连接到最后一个块的隐私头的准确性来衡量。在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法有效最小化了私有信息流，同时实现了高稀疏性。

    arXiv:2608.13773v1 Announce Type: cross  Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications with growing privacy leakage concerns. This poses significant privacy risks when deploying models that process sensitive attributes. In this context, we propose CutClean, a privacy-aware pruning method that allows to reduce privacy information flow through the network, while increasing its sparsity. Our approach employs auxiliary linear privacy heads placed at each network's block to quantify information leakage, and further applies increasing levels of sparsity to remove the private attribute leakage, measured in terms of the accuracy of the privacy head attached to the last block. This review surveys a broad and rapidly evolving toolkit of data-driven techniques for translational neuroscience and personalized neuro-health, organized around four complementary methodological pillars. Throughout, we emphasize how these methodologically diverse approaches converge on a common translational goal: personalized, mechanistically grounded, and clinically actionable models of individual brain health, and we close by discussing the principal open statistical, computational, and clinical challenges that remain.

GALA is a two-stage approach that first contrastively couples a pretrained text encoder with a time-series foundation model into a shared embedding space with both encoders adapted to generation by an auxiliary generative loss, and then freezes the resulting caption embedding to drive a flow-matching generator.     该论文报告了一个AI辅助开发项目的成本测量案例，发现初始19.4倍成本比率因两个测量错误被高估约2倍，修正后为9.9倍，并强调成本测量中的潜在陷阱。

We instrumented development with a three-layer cost model (real AI spend, self-reported human effort, human counterfactual) and initially reported a 19.4x cost ratio. A follow-up pass revealed two independent errors -- inferring per-token cost under a flat-rate subscription, and pricing the counterfactual with the wrong regional labor rates -- that together had inflated the ratio by roughly 2x; the corrected figure is ~9.9x.
